<file><path>PHASE_10_COMPLETION_SUMMARY.md</path>
<content lines="1-422">
  1 | # Phase 10: Authentication Overhaul - Completion Summary
  2 | 
  3 | **Date:** December 3, 2025
  4 | **Status:** ✅ **COMPLETE** (13/14 tasks - 92%)
  5 | **Remaining:** Manual testing required
  6 | 
  7 | ---
  8 | 
  9 | ## 📊 Overview
 10 | 
 11 | Phase 10 successfully implements a comprehensive authentication system that separates customer and employee authentication flows, adds Google OAuth for customers, implements 2FA for employees, and provides granular permission-based authorization.
 12 | 
 13 | ### Completion Status
 14 | - ✅ Backend Implementation: **100% Complete**
 15 | - ✅ Frontend Implementation: **100% Complete**
 16 | - ⏳ Testing: **Pending manual verification**
 17 | 
 18 | ---
 19 | 
 20 | ## 🎯 Key Features Implemented
 21 | 
 22 | ### 1. **Separate Authentication Flows**
 23 | - **Customer Login** (`/customer/login`): Email/password + Google OAuth
 24 | - **Employee Login** (`/employee/login`): Email/password + 2FA (TOTP)
 25 | - **Customer Dashboard** (`/dashboard`): Personalized landing page
 26 | - Different session durations: Customers (30 days), Employees (varies by role)
 27 | 
 28 | ### 2. **Google OAuth Integration**
 29 | - Google Sign-In button on customer login page
 30 | - Auto-create or link OAuth accounts to existing emails
 31 | - Trust Google's email verification
 32 | - Uses `@react-oauth/google` library
 33 | 
 34 | ### 3. **Two-Factor Authentication (2FA)**
 35 | - TOTP-based 2FA using Google Authenticator
 36 | - QR code generation for easy setup
 37 | - 10 backup codes for recovery
 38 | - Required for all employees (can be made optional per role)
 39 | - Graceful 2-step login flow
 40 | 
 41 | ### 4. **Refresh Token System**
 42 | - 1-hour access tokens
 43 | - 30-day refresh tokens
 44 | - Device tracking (IP, user agent, device name)
 45 | - Revocation support (logout single device or all devices)
 46 | - Token blacklisting for proper logout
 47 | 
 48 | ### 5. **Auto-Refresh Mechanism**
 49 | - Automatic token refresh on 401 errors
 50 | - Request queuing during refresh
 51 | - Seamless user experience (no re-login required)
 52 | - Redirects to appropriate login page on refresh failure
 53 | 
 54 | ### 6. **Permission-Based Authorization**
 55 | - 20 granular permissions across 6 categories
 56 | - Role-permission mappings (Admin: 20, Manager: 17, Cashier: 7, Staff: 2)
 57 | - Permission checking in backend routes
 58 | - Extensible system for future permissions
 59 | 
 60 | ### 7. **Audit Logging**
 61 | - Comprehensive logging of all authentication events
 62 | - Tracks: login attempts, 2FA verifications, token refreshes, logouts
 63 | - Stores: IP address, user agent, device info, success/failure status
 64 | - Useful for security monitoring and compliance
 65 | 
 66 | ### 8. **Account Security**
 67 | - Password strength validation (8+ chars, uppercase, lowercase, number, special char)
 68 | - Account lockout after 5 failed attempts (30-minute duration)
 69 | - Email verification token system (TODO: implement email sending)
 70 | - Password reset token system (TODO: implement email sending)
 71 | 
 72 | ---
 73 | 
 74 | ## 🗂️ Files Created/Modified
 75 | 
 76 | ### Backend Files
 77 | 
 78 | #### Created:
 79 | 1. **`backend/migrations/007_authentication_overhaul.sql`** (614 lines)
 80 |    - 6 new tables: `refresh_tokens`, `token_blacklist`, `auth_audit_log`, `permissions`, `role_permissions`, `employee_sessions`
 81 |    - Modified 2 tables: `customers` (OAuth fields), `employees` (2FA fields)
 82 |    - Seeded 20 permissions and 46 role-permission mappings
 83 | 
 84 | 2. **`backend/services/auth_service.py`** (~1,048 lines)
 85 |    - `register_customer()`, `login_customer()`, `login_customer_google_oauth()`
 86 |    - `login_employee()`, `login_employee_pin()`, `enable_2fa()`, `verify_and_enable_2fa()`, `disable_2fa()`
 87 |    - `refresh_access_token()`, `logout()`, `logout_all_devices()`
 88 |    - `has_permission()`, `get_employee_permissions()`
 89 |    - Helper methods for token generation, TOTP verification, password validation
 90 | 
 91 | 3. **`backend/routes/auth_routes.py`** (~680 lines)
 92 |    - 20 API endpoints covering all authentication operations
 93 |    - Customer: register, login, google-oauth, verify-email, forgot-password, reset-password, me
 94 |    - Employee: login, pin-login, enable-2fa, verify-2fa, disable-2fa
 95 |    - Token: refresh, logout, logout-all, sessions (list/delete)
 96 |    - Admin: permissions (list), audit-log (view)
 97 | 
 98 | #### Modified:
 99 | 4. **`backend/models/database_models.py`**
100 |    - Added 6 new model classes: `RefreshToken`, `TokenBlacklist`, `AuthAuditLog`, `Permission`, `RolePermission`, `EmployeeSession`
101 |    - Fixed: Renamed `metadata` column to `extra_data` (SQLAlchemy reserved word conflict)
102 | 
103 | 5. **`backend/models/__init__.py`**
104 |    - Exported 6 new models for use throughout application
105 | 
106 | 6. **`backend/app.py`**
107 |    - Registered `auth_bp` blueprint with `/api/auth` prefix
108 | 
109 | 7. **`backend/requirements.txt`**
110 |    - Added: `pyotp==2.9.0`, `qrcode[pil]==7.4.2`, `google-auth==2.35.0`, `google-auth-oauthlib==1.2.1`, `requests==2.32.3`
111 | 
112 | ### Frontend Files
113 | 
114 | #### Created:
115 | 8. **`frontend/src/pages/CustomerLogin.js`** (~165 lines)
116 |    - Email/password form with validation
117 |    - Google OAuth button integration
118 |    - Links to register, employee login, forgot password
119 |    - Auto-redirect to `/dashboard` on success
120 | 
121 | 9. **`frontend/src/pages/CustomerDashboard.js`** (~125 lines)
122 |    - Welcome message with user's first name
123 |    - Quick actions: Shop Now, My Orders, Wishlist, Cart
124 |    - Account information display
125 |    - Recent orders preview (empty state)
126 | 
127 | 10. **`frontend/src/styles/Dashboard.css`** (~190 lines)
128 |     - Clean, minimalist styling matching brand
129 |     - Responsive grid layout
130 |     - Action cards with hover effects
131 | 
132 | 11. **`frontend/src/pages/EmployeeLogin.js`** (~210 lines)
133 |     - 2-step login flow (email/password → 2FA code)
134 |     - Conditional 2FA input based on backend response
135 |     - Monospace input styling for 6-digit TOTP code
136 |     - Role-based redirect (Admin/Manager → /admin, Cashier → /pos)
137 | 
138 | 12. **`frontend/.env`**

142 | #### Modified:

144 |     - Added: `loginCustomer()`, `loginCustomerWithGoogle()`, `loginEmployee()`
145 |     - Store refresh tokens in localStorage

147 | 
148 | 14. **`frontend/src/services/api.js`**
149 |     - Added: `customerGoogleOAuth()`, `refreshToken()`, `logoutUser()`
150 |     - Implemented auto-refresh interceptor (100+ lines)
151 |     - Request queuing during token refresh
152 |     - Automatic logout and redirect on refresh failure
153 | 
154 | 15. **`frontend/src/App.js`**
155 |     - Wrapped app with `GoogleOAuthProvider`
156 |     - Added routes: `/customer/login`, `/dashboard`, `/employee/login`
157 |     - Imported new page components
158 | 
159 | ---
160 | 
161 | ## 🔐 Security Features
162 | 
163 | ### Implemented:
164 | - ✅ Password hashing with Werkzeug scrypt
165 | - ✅ HTTPS-only cookies (production setting in Flask)
166 | - ✅ JWT tokens with short expiration (1 hour access, 30 days refresh)
167 | - ✅ Token blacklisting on logout
168 | - ✅ Account lockout after failed attempts
169 | - ✅ TOTP 2FA for employees
170 | - ✅ Backup codes for 2FA recovery
171 | - ✅ Comprehensive audit logging
172 | - ✅ GDPR consent tracking
173 | - ✅ Data encryption for PII (existing in Customer model)
174 | - ✅ Permission-based authorization
175 | 
176 | ### To Be Implemented (Future):
177 | - ⏳ Email verification (token system exists, needs email sending)
178 | - ⏳ Password reset via email (token system exists, needs email sending)
179 | - ⏳ Rate limiting on authentication endpoints
180 | - ⏳ IP whitelisting for admin users
181 | - ⏳ Session timeout warnings
182 | - ⏳ Suspicious login detection
183 | 
184 | ---
185 | 
186 | ## 📡 API Endpoints
187 | 
188 | ### Customer Authentication:
189 | ```
190 | POST   /api/auth/customer/register          # Register new customer
191 | POST   /api/auth/customer/login             # Login with email/password
192 | POST   /api/auth/customer/google-oauth      # Login/register with Google
193 | GET    /api/auth/customer/me                # Get current customer profile
194 | POST   /api/auth/customer/verify-email      # Verify email (TODO: implement)
195 | POST   /api/auth/customer/forgot-password   # Request password reset (TODO)
196 | POST   /api/auth/customer/reset-password    # Reset password (TODO)
197 | ```
198 | 
199 | ### Employee Authentication:
200 | ```
201 | POST   /api/auth/employee/login             # Login with email/password (+ optional 2FA)
202 | POST   /api/auth/employee/pin-login         # Quick PIN login for POS
203 | POST   /api/auth/employee/enable-2fa        # Generate QR code and backup codes
204 | POST   /api/auth/employee/verify-2fa        # Verify TOTP code and enable 2FA
205 | POST   /api/auth/employee/disable-2fa       # Disable 2FA (requires current TOTP)
206 | GET    /api/auth/employee/me                # Get current employee profile
207 | ```
208 | 
209 | ### Token Management:
210 | ```
211 | POST   /api/auth/refresh                    # Refresh access token
212 | POST   /api/auth/logout                     # Logout (blacklist tokens)
213 | POST   /api/auth/logout-all                 # Logout from all devices
214 | GET    /api/auth/sessions                   # List active sessions
215 | DELETE /api/auth/sessions/:id              # Revoke specific session
216 | ```
217 | 
218 | ### Admin:
219 | ```
220 | GET    /api/auth/permissions                # List all permissions (admin only)
221 | GET    /api/auth/audit-log                  # View audit log (admin only)
222 | ```
223 | 
224 | ---
225 | 
226 | ## 🧪 Testing Checklist
227 | 
228 | ### Customer Authentication Flow:
229 | - [ ] Register new customer with email/password
230 | - [ ] Verify password strength validation works
231 | - [ ] Login with email/password
232 | - [ ] Login with Google OAuth (new account)
233 | - [ ] Login with Google OAuth (existing account)
234 | - [ ] Access customer dashboard after login
235 | - [ ] Verify token auto-refresh works on expired token
236 | - [ ] Logout and verify redirect to login page
237 | 
238 | ### Employee Authentication Flow:
239 | - [ ] Login employee without 2FA enabled
240 | - [ ] Enable 2FA for employee
241 | - [ ] Scan QR code with Google Authenticator
242 | - [ ] Login employee with 2FA (enter TOTP code)
243 | - [ ] Verify backup code works for 2FA
244 | - [ ] Test account lockout after 5 failed attempts
245 | - [ ] Verify lockout clears after 30 minutes
246 | - [ ] Disable 2FA for employee
247 | - [ ] Test role-based redirect (Admin → /admin, Cashier → /pos)
248 | 
249 | ### Token Management:
250 | - [ ] Verify access token expires after 1 hour
251 | - [ ] Verify auto-refresh works seamlessly
252 | - [ ] Test logout (single device)
253 | - [ ] Test logout all devices
254 | - [ ] Verify blacklisted tokens are rejected
255 | 
256 | ### Permission System:
257 | - [ ] Verify Admin has all 20 permissions
258 | - [ ] Verify Manager has 17 permissions
259 | - [ ] Verify Cashier has 7 permissions
260 | - [ ] Verify Staff has 2 permissions
261 | - [ ] Test permission-based route access
262 | 
263 | ### Security:
264 | - [ ] Verify passwords are hashed in database
265 | - [ ] Verify TOTP secrets are encrypted
266 | - [ ] Verify audit log captures all events
267 | - [ ] Test CSRF protection (if enabled)
268 | - [ ] Verify refresh tokens are device-specific
269 | 
270 | ---
271 | 
272 | ## 🚀 Deployment Checklist
273 | 
274 | ### Backend:
275 | 1. ✅ Install new Python packages: `pip install -r requirements.txt`
276 | 2. ✅ Run migration: `psql -U postgres -d happy_place_db -f migrations/007_authentication_overhaul.sql`
277 | 3. ⏳ Set environment variable: `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` (from Google Cloud Console)
278 | 4. ⏳ Update Flask config: `JWT_SECRET_KEY` (generate strong random key)
279 | 5. ⏳ Enable HTTPS in production (for secure cookies)
280 | 
281 | ### Frontend:
282 | 1. ✅ Install new npm package: `npm install @react-oauth/google`
283 | 2. ⏳ Set environment variable: `REACT_APP_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` in `.env`
284 | 3. ⏳ Build production bundle: `npm run build`
285 | 4. ⏳ Deploy static files to CDN/web server
286 | 
287 | ### Google Cloud Console:
288 | 1. ⏳ Create OAuth 2.0 credentials
289 | 2. ⏳ Add authorized redirect URIs
290 | 3. ⏳ Copy Client ID to backend and frontend `.env` files
291 | 
292 | ---
293 | 
294 | ## 📈 Code Statistics
295 | 
296 | ### Backend:
297 | - **Lines of code:** ~2,582
298 |   - `auth_service.py`: ~1,048 lines
299 |   - `auth_routes.py`: ~680 lines
300 |   - `database_models.py`: ~240 lines (additions)
301 |   - `007_authentication_overhaul.sql`: ~614 lines
302 | 
303 | ### Frontend:
304 | - **Lines of code:** ~890
305 |   - `CustomerLogin.js`: ~165 lines
306 |   - `EmployeeLogin.js`: ~210 lines
307 |   - `CustomerDashboard.js`: ~125 lines
308 |   - `Dashboard.css`: ~190 lines
309 |   - `api.js` (interceptor): ~100 lines (additions)
310 |   - `AuthContext.js`: ~50 lines (additions)
311 |   - `App.js`: ~50 lines (additions)
312 | 
313 | ### Total:
314 | - **~3,472 lines of code** added/modified for Phase 10
315 | 
316 | ---
317 | 
318 | ## 🔧 Configuration Required
319 | 
320 | ### 1. Google OAuth Setup
321 | You need to create OAuth 2.0 credentials in Google Cloud Console:
322 | 
323 | 1. Go to: https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials
324 | 2. Create new OAuth 2.0 Client ID
325 | 3. Application type: Web application
326 | 4. Authorized JavaScript origins:
327 |    ```
328 |    http://localhost:3000
329 |    https://yourdomain.com
330 |    ```
331 | 5. Authorized redirect URIs:
332 |    ```
333 |    http://localhost:3000
334 |    https://yourdomain.com
335 |    ```
336 | 6. Copy the Client ID
337 | 
338 | ### 2. Environment Variables
339 | 
340 | **Backend** (`backend/.env` or `backend/config.py`):
341 | ```python
342 | GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID = "your_google_client_id_here.apps.googleusercontent.com"
343 | JWT_SECRET_KEY = "your_super_secret_jwt_key_here"  # Generate: secrets.token_hex(32)
344 | ```
345 | 
346 | **Frontend** (`frontend/.env`):
347 | ```bash
348 | REACT_APP_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=your_google_client_id_here.apps.googleusercontent.com
349 | REACT_APP_API_URL=http://localhost:5001/api
350 | ```
351 | 
352 | ---
353 | 
354 | ## 🐛 Known Issues / Future Improvements
355 | 
356 | ### To Fix:

358 | 2. **Password Reset:** Backend endpoints exist but need email integration
359 | 3. **EmployeeLogin API Bug:** Need to verify the backend returns `requires_2fa` flag correctly
360 | 
361 | ### Future Enhancements:
362 | 1. **Remember Me:** Optional longer session for customers (90 days)
363 | 2. **Social Login:** Add Facebook, Apple Sign-In
364 | 3. **Magic Link Login:** Passwordless authentication via email
365 | 4. **Biometric Auth:** Face ID / Touch ID for mobile app
366 | 5. **Session Management UI:** Let users view and revoke active sessions
367 | 6. **Activity Log:** Show users their recent login history
368 | 7. **Security Alerts:** Email notifications for suspicious logins
369 | 8. **Multi-tenancy:** Support for multiple store locations with separate admin access
370 | 
371 | ---
372 | 
373 | ## 📚 Documentation
374 | 
375 | ### For Developers:
376 | - See `backend/routes/auth_routes.py` for API endpoint documentation
377 | - See `backend/services/auth_service.py` for authentication business logic
378 | - See `PHASE_10_PROGRESS_REPORT.md` for detailed implementation notes
379 | 
380 | ### For Users:
381 | - **Customer Guide:** Login at `/customer/login` or use "Login with Google"
382 | - **Employee Guide:** Login at `/employee/login` with 2FA enabled
383 | - **2FA Setup:** Contact admin to enable 2FA, then scan QR code with Google Authenticator
384 |

405 | ## 🎉 Summary

408 | 1. Manual testing of all authentication flows

<content lines="1-901">
  1 | """
  2 | POS API Routes
  3 | Handles all Point of Sale endpoints
  4 | """
  5 | 
  6 | from flask import request, jsonify
  7 | from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required, get_jwt_identity, get_jwt
  8 | from functools import wraps
  9 | 
 10 | from routes import api
 11 | from services.pos_service import POSService
 12 | from services.receipt_service import ReceiptService
 13 | from logging_utils import get_logger, safe_auth_context
 14 | 
 15 | # Initialize logger
 16 | logger = get_logger(__name__)
 17 | 
 18 | 
 19 | def employee_required(fn):
 20 |     """Decorator to require employee authentication"""
 21 |     @wraps(fn)
 22 |     @jwt_required()
 23 |     def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
 24 |         try:
 25 |             claims = get_jwt()
 26 |             logger.debug(f"employee_required - user_type: {claims.get('user_type')}")
 27 | 
 28 |             # Check if user_type is employee
 29 |             if claims.get('user_type') != 'employee':
 30 |                 return jsonify({
 31 |                     'success': False,
 32 |                     'error': 'Employee access required'
 33 |                 }), 403
 34 | 
 35 |             return fn(*args, **kwargs)
 36 |         except Exception as e:
 37 |             logger.error(
 38 |                 "Employee authentication decorator failed",
 39 |                 extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
 40 |                     user_type='employee',
 41 |                     ip=request.remote_addr,
 42 |                     user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent')
 43 |                 )},
 44 |                 exc_info=True,
 45 |             )
 46 |             return jsonify({
 47 |                 'success': False,
 48 |                 'error': 'Authentication error'
 49 |             }), 422
 50 |     return wrapper
 51 | 
 52 | 
 53 | def manager_required(fn):
 54 |     """Decorator to require manager or admin role"""
 55 |     @wraps(fn)
 56 |     @jwt_required()
 57 |     def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
 58 |         claims = get_jwt()
 59 | 
 60 |         # Check if user_type is employee
 61 |         if claims.get('user_type') != 'employee':
 62 |             return jsonify({
 63 |                 'success': False,
 64 |                 'error': 'Employee access required'
 65 |             }), 403
 66 | 
 67 |         # Check if role is manager or admin
 68 |         role = claims.get('role')
 69 |         if role not in ['manager', 'admin']:
 70 |             return jsonify({
 71 |                 'success': False,
 72 |                 'error': 'Manager or admin access required'
 73 |             }), 403
 74 | 
 75 |         return fn(*args, **kwargs)
 76 |     return wrapper
 77 | 
 78 | 
 79 | # ============================================================================
 80 | # TRANSACTION ENDPOINTS
 81 | # ============================================================================
 82 | 
 83 | @api.route('/pos/transactions', methods=['POST'])
 84 | @employee_required
 85 | def create_transaction():
 86 |     """
 87 |     Create a new POS transaction
 88 | 
 89 |     POST /api/pos/transactions
 90 |     Body: {
 91 |         "shift_id": 1,
 92 |         "payment_method": "cash",
 93 |         "items": [
 94 |             {"variant_id": 21, "quantity": 2},
 95 |             {"variant_id": 35, "quantity": 1}
 96 |         ],
 97 |         "customer_id": 123 (optional),
 98 |         "cash_tendered": 10000.00 (required for cash)
 99 |     }
100 |     """
101 |     try:
102 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
103 |         data = request.get_json()
104 | 
105 |         # Validate required fields
106 |         if not data.get('shift_id'):
107 |             return jsonify({
108 |                 'success': False,
109 |                 'error': 'shift_id is required'
110 |             }), 400
111 | 
112 |         if not data.get('payment_method'):
113 |             return jsonify({
114 |                 'success': False,
115 |                 'error': 'payment_method is required'
116 |             }), 400
117 | 
118 |         if not data.get('items') or len(data.get('items')) == 0:
119 |             return jsonify({
120 |                 'success': False,
121 |                 'error': 'items array is required and must not be empty'
122 |             }), 400
123 | 
124 |         # Validate cash tendered for cash payments
125 |         if data.get('payment_method') == 'cash' and not data.get('cash_tendered'):
126 |             return jsonify({
127 |                 'success': False,
128 |                 'error': 'cash_tendered is required for cash payments'
129 |             }), 400
130 | 
131 |         # Get store location from shift (we'll get this from the shift)
132 |         # For now, use store_location_id from request or default to 1
133 |         store_location_id = data.get('store_location_id', 1)
134 | 
135 |         result = POSService.create_transaction(
136 |             employee_id=employee_id,
137 |             store_location_id=store_location_id,
138 |             shift_id=data['shift_id'],
139 |             payment_method=data['payment_method'],
140 |             items=data['items'],
141 |             customer_id=data.get('customer_id'),
142 |             cash_tendered=data.get('cash_tendered')
143 |         )
144 | 
145 |         if result.get('success'):
146 |             return jsonify(result), 201
147 |         else:
148 |             return jsonify(result), 400
149 | 
150 |     except Exception as e:
151 |         logger.error(
152 |             "POS operation failed",
153 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
154 |                 user_type='employee',
155 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
156 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent')
157 |             )},
158 |             exc_info=True,
159 |         )
160 |         return jsonify({
161 |             'success': False,
162 |             'error': 'Internal server error'
163 |         }), 500
164 | 
165 |

172 |     GET /api/pos/transactions/:id

176 | 
177 |         if transaction:

182 |         else:
183 |             return jsonify({

185 |                 'error': 'Transaction not found'
186 |             }), 404
187 | 
188 |     except Exception as e:
189 |         logger.error(
190 |             "POS operation failed",
191 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
192 |                 user_type='employee',
193 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
194 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent')
195 |             )},
196 |             exc_info=True,
197 |         )
198 |         return jsonify({
199 |             'success': False,
200 |             'error': 'Internal server error'
201 |         }), 500

204 | @api.route('/pos/transactions/<int:transaction_id>/void', methods=['POST'])
205 | @manager_required
206 | def void_transaction(transaction_id):
207 |     """
208 |     Void a transaction (manager/admin only)
209 | 
210 |     POST /api/pos/transactions/:id/void
211 |     Body: {
212 |         "void_reason": "Customer returned all items"
213 |     }
214 |     """
215 |     try:
216 |         voided_by = int(get_jwt_identity())
217 |         data = request.get_json()
218 |         void_reason = data.get('void_reason')
219 | 
220 |         if not void_reason:
221 |             return jsonify({
222 |                 'success': False,
223 |                 'error': 'void_reason is required'
224 |             }), 400
225 | 
226 |         result = POSService.void_transaction(
227 |             transaction_id=transaction_id,
228 |             voided_by=voided_by,
229 |             void_reason=void_reason
230 |         )
231 | 
232 |         if result.get('success'):
233 |             return jsonify(result), 200
234 |         else:
235 |             return jsonify(result), 400
236 | 
237 |     except Exception as e:
238 |         logger.error(
239 |             "POS operation failed",
240 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
241 |                 user_type='employee',
242 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
243 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent')
244 |             )},
245 |             exc_info=True,
246 |         )
247 |         return jsonify({
248 |             'success': False,
249 |             'error': 'Internal server error'
250 |         }), 500
251 |

253 | @api.route('/pos/transactions/today', methods=['GET'])
254 | @employee_required
255 | def get_todays_transactions():
256 |     """
257 |     Get all transactions for today
258 | 
259 |     GET /api/pos/transactions/today?shift_id=1&store_location_id=1
260 |     """
261 |     try:
262 |         shift_id = request.args.get('shift_id', type=int)
263 |         store_location_id = request.args.get('store_location_id', type=int)

265 |         transactions = POSService.get_todays_transactions(

276 |     except Exception as e:

278 |             "POS operation failed",

282 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent')
283 |             )},
284 |             exc_info=True,

286 |         return jsonify({

675 | @api.route('/pos/cash-movements', methods=['POST'])
676 | @employee_required
677 | def record_cash_movement():
678 |     """
679 |     Record cash in/out movement
680 | 
681 |     POST /api/pos/cash-movements
682 |     Body: {
683 |         "shift_id": 1,
684 |         "movement_type": "cash_in" or "cash_out",
685 |         "amount": 1000.00,
686 |         "reason": "Petty cash withdrawal"
687 |     }
688 |     """
689 |     try:
690 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
691 |         data = request.get_json()
692 | 
693 |         # Validate required fields
694 |         if not data.get('shift_id'):
695 |             return jsonify({
696 |                 'success': False,
697 |                 'error': 'shift_id is required'
698 |             }), 400
699 | 
700 |         if not data.get('movement_type'):
701 |             return jsonify({
702 |                 'success': False,
703 |                 'error': 'movement_type is required'
704 |             }), 400
705 | 
706 |         if data.get('movement_type') not in ['cash_in', 'cash_out']:
707 |             return jsonify({
708 |                 'success': False,
709 |                 'error': 'movement_type must be cash_in or cash_out'
710 |             }), 400
711 | 
712 |         if data.get('amount') is None:
713 |             return jsonify({
714 |                 'success': False,
715 |                 'error': 'amount is required'
716 |             }), 400
717 | 
718 |         if not data.get('reason'):
719 |             return jsonify({
720 |                 'success': False,
721 |                 'error': 'reason is required'
722 |             }), 400
723 | 
724 |         movement_id = POSService.record_cash_movement(
725 |             shift_id=data['shift_id'],
726 |             movement_type=data['movement_type'],
727 |             amount=data['amount'],
728 |             reason=data['reason'],
729 |             performed_by=employee_id
730 |         )
731 | 
732 |         return jsonify({
733 |             'success': True,
734 |             'movement_id': movement_id,
735 |             'message': 'Cash movement recorded'
736 |         }), 201
737 | 
738 |     except Exception as e:
739 |         logger.error(
740 |             "POS operation failed",
741 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
742 |                 user_type='employee',
743 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
744 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent')
745 |             )},
746 |             exc_info=True,
747 |         )
748 |         return jsonify({
749 |             'success': False,
750 |             'error': 'Internal server error'
751 |         }), 500

758 | @api.route('/pos/transactions/<int:transaction_id>/receipt/thermal', methods=['GET'])
759 | @employee_required
760 | def get_thermal_receipt(transaction_id):
761 |     """
762 |     Get thermal receipt for printing
763 | 
764 |     GET /api/pos/transactions/:id/receipt/thermal?width=58
765 |     Query params:
766 |         - width: 58 (default) or 80 (for 80mm paper)
767 |     """
768 |     try:
769 |         # Get transaction
770 |         transaction = POSService.get_transaction(transaction_id)
771 | 
772 |         if not transaction:
773 |             return jsonify({
774 |                 'success': False,
775 |                 'error': 'Transaction not found'
776 |             }), 404
777 | 
778 |         # Get width parameter (default 58mm = 32 chars)
779 |         width_mm = request.args.get('width', '58')
780 |         char_width = 32 if width_mm == '58' else 48
781 | 
782 |         # Generate receipt
783 |         receipt_text = ReceiptService.generate_thermal_receipt(transaction, width=char_width)
784 | 
785 |         return jsonify({
786 |             'success': True,
787 |             'receipt': receipt_text,
788 |             'width': width_mm
789 |         }), 200
790 | 
791 |     except Exception as e:
792 |         logger.error(
793 |             "POS operation failed",
794 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
795 |                 user_type='employee',
796 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
797 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent')
798 |             )},
799 |             exc_info=True,
800 |         )
801 |         return jsonify({
802 |             'success': False,
803 |             'error': 'Internal server error'
804 |         }), 500
805 |

<content lines="1-2845">
   1 | """
   2 | Admin Dashboard API Routes
   3 | 
   4 | Comprehensive admin panel endpoints for:
   5 | - Dashboard metrics and analytics
   6 | - Inventory management
   7 | - Order management
   8 | - Customer management (with GDPR compliance)
   9 | - Employee management
  10 | - Promotion management
  11 | - Reports and analytics
  12 | - Store settings
  13 | 
  14 | All endpoints require JWT authentication and appropriate role permissions.
  15 | """
  16 | 
  17 | from flask import request, jsonify, Blueprint
  18 | from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required, get_jwt_identity, get_jwt
  19 | from functools import wraps
  20 | 
  21 | from services.admin_dashboard_service import AdminDashboardService
  22 | from services.inventory_management_service import InventoryManagementService
  23 | from services.order_management_service import OrderManagementService
  24 | from services.customer_management_service import CustomerManagementService
  25 | from services.employee_management_service import EmployeeManagementService
  26 | from services.promotion_service import PromotionService
  27 | from services.report_service import ReportService
  28 | from services.settings_service import SettingsService
  29 | from middleware import manager_required, admin_required
  30 | from logging_utils import get_logger, safe_auth_context
  31 | 
  32 | # Initialize logger
  33 | logger = get_logger(__name__)
  34 | 
  35 | # Create blueprint
  36 | admin_bp = Blueprint('admin', __name__, url_prefix='/api/admin')
  37 | 
  38 | # Services will be initialized in each endpoint to avoid app context issues
  39 | def get_services():
  40 |     """Get service instances (lazy initialization)"""
  41 |     return {
  42 |         'dashboard': AdminDashboardService(),
  43 |         'inventory': InventoryManagementService(),
  44 |         'order': OrderManagementService(),
  45 |         'customer': CustomerManagementService(),
  46 |         'employee': EmployeeManagementService(),
  47 |         'promotion': PromotionService(),
  48 |         'report': ReportService(),
  49 |         'settings': SettingsService()
  50 |     }
  51 | 
  52 | 
  53 | # =====================================================
  54 | # DASHBOARD ENDPOINTS
  55 | # =====================================================
  56 | 
  57 | @admin_bp.route('/dashboard/metrics', methods=['GET'])
  58 | @jwt_required()
  59 | @manager_required
  60 | def get_dashboard_metrics(current_employee):
  61 |     """
  62 |     Get key dashboard metrics (sales, orders, customers, inventory).
  63 | 
  64 |     GET /api/admin/dashboard/metrics?period=today|week|month|year
  65 | 
  66 |     Returns:
  67 |         200: Dashboard metrics
  68 |         400: Error
  69 |     """
  70 |     try:
  71 |         period = request.args.get('period', 'today')
  72 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
  73 | 
  74 |         result = get_services()['dashboard'].get_dashboard_metrics(period=period)
  75 |         return jsonify(result), 200
  76 |     except Exception as e:
  77 |         logger.error(
  78 |             "Dashboard metrics operation failed",
  79 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
  80 |                 user_type='employee',
  81 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
  82 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent'),
  83 |                 extra={"employee_id": int(get_jwt_identity()) if get_jwt_identity() else None}
  84 |             )},
  85 |             exc_info=True,
  86 |         )
  87 |         return jsonify({'error': 'Internal server error'}), 400
  88 | 
  89 | 
  90 | @admin_bp.route('/dashboard/activity', methods=['GET'])
  91 | @jwt_required()
  92 | @manager_required
  93 | def get_dashboard_activity(current_employee):
  94 |     """
  95 |     Get recent activity feed (orders, customers, inventory changes).
  96 | 
  97 |     GET /api/admin/dashboard/activity?limit=20
  98 | 
  99 |     Returns:
 100 |         200: Activity feed
 101 |         400: Error
 102 |     """
 103 |     try:
 104 |         limit = request.args.get('limit', 20, type=int)
 105 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
 106 | 
 107 |         result = get_services()['dashboard'].get_recent_activity(limit=limit)
 108 |         return jsonify(result), 200
 109 |     except Exception as e:
 110 |         logger.error(
 111 |             "Dashboard metrics operation failed",
 112 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
 113 |                 user_type='employee',
 114 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
 115 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent'),
 116 |                 extra={"employee_id": int(get_jwt_identity()) if get_jwt_identity() else None}
 117 |             )},
 118 |             exc_info=True,
 119 |         )
 120 |         return jsonify({'error': 'Internal server error'}), 400
 121 | 
 122 | 
 123 | @admin_bp.route('/dashboard/alerts', methods=['GET'])
 124 | @jwt_required()
 125 | @manager_required
 126 | def get_dashboard_alerts(current_employee):
 127 |     """
 128 |     Get system alerts (low stock, pending orders, issues).
 129 | 
 130 |     GET /api/admin/dashboard/alerts
 131 | 
 132 |     Returns:
 133 |         200: System alerts
 134 |         400: Error
 135 |     """
 136 |     try:
 137 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
 138 | 
 139 |         result = get_services()['dashboard'].get_alerts()
 140 |         return jsonify(result), 200
 141 |     except Exception as e:
 142 |         logger.error(
 143 |             "Dashboard metrics operation failed",
 144 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
 145 |                 user_type='employee',
 146 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
 147 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent'),
 148 |                 extra={"employee_id": int(get_jwt_identity()) if get_jwt_identity() else None}
 149 |             )},
 150 |             exc_info=True,
 151 |         )
 152 |         return jsonify({'error': 'Internal server error'}), 400
 153 | 
 154 | 
 155 | @admin_bp.route('/dashboard/trends', methods=['GET'])
 156 | @jwt_required()
 157 | @manager_required
 158 | def get_dashboard_trends(current_employee):
 159 |     """
 160 |     Get trends data for charts (sales, orders, revenue over time).
 161 | 
 162 |     GET /api/admin/dashboard/trends?period=week|month|year
 163 |

 563 | @admin_bp.route('/inventory', methods=['POST'])
 564 | @admin_bp.route('/products', methods=['POST'])  # Alias for backwards compatibility
 565 | @jwt_required()
 566 | @manager_required
 567 | def create_product(current_employee):
 568 |     """
 569 |     Create a new product.
 570 | 
 571 |     POST /api/admin/inventory (or /api/admin/products)
 572 |     Body: {
 573 |         "name": "Product Name",
 574 |         "description": "Description",
 575 |         "category_id": 2,
 576 |         "price": 29.99,
 577 |         "sku": "SKU-001",
 578 |         "variants": [optional array of variants]
 579 |     }
 580 | 
 581 |     Returns:
 582 |         201: Product created
 583 |         400: Error
 584 |     """
 585 |     try:
 586 |         from models.database_models import db, Product, ProductVariant, Inventory
 587 | 
 588 |         data = request.get_json()
 589 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
 590 | 
 591 |         # Required fields
 592 |         required = ['name', 'category_id', 'price']
 593 |         for field in required:
 594 |             if field not in data:
 595 |                 return jsonify({'error': f'Missing required field: {field}'}), 400
 596 | 
 597 |         # Create product
 598 |         product = Product(
 599 |             name=data['name'],
 600 |             description=data.get('description', ''),
 601 |             category_id=data['category_id'],
 602 |             price=data['price'],
 603 |             is_active=True
 604 |         )
 605 |         db.session.add(product)
 606 |         db.session.flush()  # Get product ID
 607 | 
 608 |         # Handle variants (if provided) or create default variant
 609 |         variants_data = data.get('variants', [])
 610 |         created_variants = []
 611 | 
 612 |         if not variants_data:
 613 |             # Create single default variant
 614 |             variant = ProductVariant(
 615 |                 product_id=product.id,
 616 |                 sku=data.get('sku', f'SKU-{product.id}'),
 617 |                 size=data.get('size', 'One Size'),
 618 |                 color=data.get('color', 'Default')
 619 |             )
 620 |             db.session.add(variant)
 621 |             db.session.flush()
 622 | 
 623 |             # Create inventory record
 624 |             inventory = Inventory(
 625 |                 variant_id=variant.id,
 626 |                 quantity=data.get('stock_quantity', 0),
 627 |                 reserved_quantity=0,
 628 |                 low_stock_threshold=data.get('low_stock_threshold', 10)
 629 |             )
 630 |             db.session.add(inventory)
 631 |             created_variants.append({'variant_id': variant.id, 'sku': variant.sku})
 632 |         else:
 633 |             # Create multiple variants
 634 |             for variant_data in variants_data:
 635 |                 variant = ProductVariant(
 636 |                     product_id=product.id,
 637 |                     sku=variant_data.get('sku', f'SKU-{product.id}-{len(created_variants)+1}'),
 638 |                     size=variant_data.get('size', 'One Size'),
 639 |                     color=variant_data.get('color', 'Default')
 640 |                 )
 641 |                 db.session.add(variant)
 642 |                 db.session.flush()
 643 | 
 644 |                 # Create inventory record
 645 |                 inventory = Inventory(
 646 |                     variant_id=variant.id,
 647 |                     quantity=variant_data.get('stock_quantity', 0),
 648 |                     reserved_quantity=0,
 649 |                     low_stock_threshold=variant_data.get('low_stock_threshold', 10)
 650 |                 )
 651 |                 db.session.add(inventory)
 652 |                 created_variants.append({'variant_id': variant.id, 'sku': variant.sku})
 653 | 
 654 |         db.session.commit()
 655 | 
 656 |         return jsonify({
 657 |             'message': 'Product created successfully',
 658 |             'product_id': product.id,
 659 |             'variants': created_variants
 660 |         }), 201
 661 |     except Exception as e:
 662 |         db.session.rollback()
 663 |         print(f"ERROR creating product: {str(e)}")
 664 |         import traceback
 665 |         traceback.print_exc()
 666 |         return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500
 667 | 
 668 | 
 669 | @admin_bp.route('/products/<int:product_id>', methods=['PATCH'])
 670 | @jwt_required()
 671 | @manager_required
 672 | def update_product(current_employee, product_id):
 673 |     """
 674 |     Update an existing product.
 675 | 
 676 |     PATCH /api/admin/products/:id
 677 |     Body: {
 678 |         "name": "Updated Product Name",
 679 |         "description": "Updated description",
 680 |         "price": 39.99,
 681 |         "category_id": 2,
 682 |         "is_active": true
 683 |     }
 684 | 
 685 |     Returns:
 686 |         200: Product updated
 687 |         404: Product not found
 688 |         400: Error
 689 |     """
 690 |     try:
 691 |         from models.database_models import db, Product
 692 | 
 693 |         data = request.get_json()
 694 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
 695 | 
 696 |         # Get product
 697 |         product = Product.query.get(product_id)
 698 |         if not product:
 699 |             return jsonify({'error': 'Product not found'}), 404
 700 | 
 701 |         # Update fields if provided
 702 |         if 'name' in data:
 703 |             product.name = data['name']
 704 |         if 'description' in data:
 705 |             product.description = data['description']
 706 |         if 'price' in data:
 707 |             product.price = data['price']
 708 |         if 'sale_price' in data:
 709 |             product.sale_price = data['sale_price']
 710 |         if 'category_id' in data:
 711 |             product.category_id = data['category_id']
 712 |         if 'is_active' in data:
 713 |             product.is_active = data['is_active']
 714 |         if 'is_featured' in data:
 715 |             product.is_featured = data['is_featured']
 716 |         if 'weight' in data:
 717 |             product.weight = data['weight']
 718 | 
 719 |         db.session.commit()
 720 | 
 721 |         return jsonify({
 722 |             'message': 'Product updated successfully',
 723 |             'product': {
 724 |                 'id': product.id,
 725 |                 'name': product.name,
 726 |                 'price': float(product.price),
 727 |                 'is_active': product.is_active
 728 |             }
 729 |         }), 200
 730 |     except Exception as e:
 731 |         db.session.rollback()
 732 |         logger.error(f"Update product failed: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
 733 |         return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 500
 734 | 
 735 | 
 736 | @admin_bp.route('/inventory/<int:product_id>/images', methods=['POST'])
 737 | @jwt_required()
 738 | @manager_required
 739 | def upload_product_images(current_employee, product_id):
 740 |     """
 741 |     Upload product images.
 742 | 
 743 |     POST /api/admin/inventory/:product_id/images
 744 |     Content-Type: multipart/form-data
 745 |     Form Data:
 746 |         - images: File[] (multiple image files)
 747 |         - primary_index: int (index of primary image, default 0)
 748 | 
 749 |     Returns:
 750 |         200: Images uploaded
 751 |         400: Error
 752 |     """
 753 |     try:
 754 |         from models.database_models import db, Product, ProductImage
 755 |         import os
 756 |         from werkzeug.utils import secure_filename
 757 |         from datetime import datetime
 758 | 
 759 |         # Check if product exists
 760 |         product = Product.query.get(product_id)
 761 |         if not product:
 762 |             return jsonify({'error': 'Product not found'}), 404
 763 | 
 764 |         # Check if files were uploaded
 765 |         if 'images' not in request.files:
 766 |             return jsonify({'error': 'No images provided'}), 400
 767 | 
 768 |         files = request.files.getlist('images')
 769 |         if len(files) == 0:
 770 |             return jsonify({'error': 'No images provided'}), 400
 771 | 
 772 |         # Get primary index
 773 |         primary_index = int(request.form.get('primary_index', 0))
 774 | 
 775 |         # Upload directory
 776 |         upload_dir = os.path.join('static', 'uploads', 'products')
 777 |         os.makedirs(upload_dir, exist_ok=True)
 778 | 
 779 |         uploaded_images = []
 780 | 
 781 |         for index, file in enumerate(files):
 782 |             if file and file.filename:
 783 |                 # Secure filename
 784 |                 filename = secure_filename(file.filename)
 785 |                 timestamp = datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
 786 |                 filename = f"product_{product_id}_{timestamp}_{index}_{filename}"
 787 | 
 788 |                 # Save file
 789 |                 filepath = os.path.join(upload_dir, filename)
 790 |                 file.save(filepath)
 791 | 
 792 |                 # Create database record
 793 |                 image_url = f"/static/uploads/products/{filename}"
 794 |                 is_primary = (index == primary_index)
 795 | 
 796 |                 # If this is the new primary, unset other primary images
 797 |                 if is_primary:
 798 |                     ProductImage.query.filter_by(
 799 |                         product_id=product_id,
 800 |                         is_primary=True
 801 |                     ).update({'is_primary': False})
 802 | 
 803 |                 product_image = ProductImage(
 804 |                     product_id=product_id,
 805 |                     image_url=image_url,
 806 |                     alt_text=product.name,
 807 |                     display_order=index,
 808 |                     is_primary=is_primary
 809 |                 )
 810 |                 db.session.add(product_image)
 811 |                 uploaded_images.append({
 812 |                     'url': image_url,
 813 |                     'is_primary': is_primary
 814 |                 })
 815 | 
 816 |         db.session.commit()
 817 | 
 818 |         return jsonify({
 819 |             'message': f'{len(uploaded_images)} images uploaded successfully',
 820 |             'images': uploaded_images
 821 |         }), 200
 822 |     except Exception as e:
 823 |         db.session.rollback()
 824 |         print(f"ERROR uploading images: {str(e)}")
 825 |         import traceback
 826 |         traceback.print_exc()
 827 |         return jsonify({'error': str(e)}), 400
 828 | 
 829 |

 831 | # ORDER MANAGEMENT ENDPOINTS
 832 | # =====================================================
 833 | 
 834 | @admin_bp.route('/orders', methods=['GET'])

 836 | @manager_required
 837 | def get_orders(current_employee):
 838 |     """
 839 |     Get orders list with filters and pagination.
 840 | 
 841 |     GET /api/admin/orders?status=&channel=&search=&page=1&limit=50
 842 | 
 843 |     Returns:
 844 |         200: Orders list

1423 | @admin_bp.route('/customers/<int:customer_id>/anonymize', methods=['POST'])
1424 | @jwt_required()
1425 | @admin_required
1426 | def anonymize_customer(current_employee, customer_id):
1427 |     """
1428 |     Anonymize customer data (GDPR right to be forgotten - Admin only).
1429 | 
1430 |     POST /api/admin/customers/:customer_id/anonymize
1431 |     Body: {
1432 |         "confirmation": "ANONYMIZE"
1433 |     }
1434 | 
1435 |     Returns:
1436 |         200: Customer anonymized
1437 |         400: Error
1438 |     """
1439 |     try:
1440 |         data = request.get_json()
1441 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
1442 | 
1443 |         if data.get('confirmation') != 'ANONYMIZE':
1444 |             return jsonify({'error': 'Confirmation required'}), 400
1445 | 
1446 |         reason = data.get('reason', 'Admin requested anonymization')
1447 |         success = get_services()['customer'].anonymize_customer(
1448 |             customer_id=customer_id,
1449 |             reason=reason,
1450 |             performed_by=employee_id
1451 |         )
1452 | 
1453 |         if success:
1454 |             return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Customer anonymized successfully'}), 200
1455 |         else:
1456 |             return jsonify({'error': 'Failed to anonymize customer'}), 400
1457 |     except Exception as e:
1458 |         logger.error(
1459 |             "Dashboard metrics operation failed",
1460 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
1461 |                 user_type='employee',
1462 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
1463 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent'),
1464 |                 extra={"employee_id": int(get_jwt_identity()) if get_jwt_identity() else None}
1465 |             )},
1466 |             exc_info=True,
1467 |         )
1468 |         return jsonify({'error': 'Internal server error'}), 400

1923 | @admin_bp.route('/promotions', methods=['POST'])
1924 | @jwt_required()
1925 | @manager_required
1926 | def create_promotion(current_employee):
1927 |     """
1928 |     Create new promotion.
1929 | 
1930 |     POST /api/admin/promotions
1931 |     Body: {
1932 |         "code": "SUMMER20",
1933 |         "name": "Summer Sale",
1934 |         "discount_type": "percentage|fixed_amount|free_shipping",
1935 |         "discount_value": 20,
1936 |         "start_date": "2025-06-01",
1937 |         "end_date": "2025-06-30",
1938 |         "minimum_order_amount": 100 (optional),
1939 |         "maximum_discount_amount": 50 (optional),
1940 |         "usage_limit": 100 (optional),
1941 |         "usage_per_customer": 1 (optional)
1942 |     }
1943 | 
1944 |     Returns:
1945 |         201: Promotion created
1946 |         400: Error
1947 |     """
1948 |     try:
1949 |         data = request.get_json()
1950 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
1951 | 
1952 |         # Map 'type' to 'discount_type' and 'value' to 'discount_value' for backwards compatibility
1953 |         if 'type' in data and 'discount_type' not in data:
1954 |             data['discount_type'] = data.pop('type')
1955 |         if 'value' in data and 'discount_value' not in data:
1956 |             data['discount_value'] = data.pop('value')
1957 | 
1958 |         required = ['code', 'name', 'discount_type', 'discount_value', 'start_date', 'end_date']
1959 |         for field in required:
1960 |             if field not in data:
1961 |                 return jsonify({'error': f'Missing required field: {field}'}), 400
1962 | 
1963 |         result = get_services()['promotion'].create_promotion(
1964 |             data=data,
1965 |             created_by=employee_id
1966 |         )
1967 | 
1968 |         if result.get('success'):
1969 |             return jsonify(result), 201
1970 |         else:
1971 |             return jsonify(result), 400
1972 |     except Exception as e:
1973 |         logger.error(
1974 |             "Dashboard metrics operation failed",
1975 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
1976 |                 user_type='employee',
1977 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
1978 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent'),
1979 |                 extra={"employee_id": int(get_jwt_identity()) if get_jwt_identity() else None}
1980 |             )},
1981 |             exc_info=True,
1982 |         )
1983 |         return jsonify({'error': 'Internal server error'}), 400
1984 |

2728 | @admin_bp.route('/settings/currency', methods=['PUT'])
2729 | @jwt_required()
2730 | @admin_required
2731 | def update_currency_settings(current_employee):
2732 |     """
2733 |     Update currency settings (Admin only).
2734 | 
2735 |     PUT /api/admin/settings/currency
2736 |     Body: {
2737 |         "currency": "KSh",
2738 |         "currency_code": "KES"
2739 |     }
2740 | 
2741 |     Supported currencies:
2742 |     - KES (Kenyan Shilling): KSh
2743 |     - USD (US Dollar): $
2744 |     - EUR (Euro): €
2745 |     - GBP (British Pound): £
2746 | 
2747 |     Returns:
2748 |         200: Currency updated
2749 |         400: Error
2750 |     """
2751 |     try:
2752 |         data = request.get_json()
2753 |         employee_id = int(get_jwt_identity())
2754 | 
2755 |         if 'currency' not in data or 'currency_code' not in data:
2756 |             return jsonify({'error': 'Currency and currency_code required'}), 400
2757 | 
2758 |         # Validate currency code
2759 |         valid_currencies = {
2760 |             'KES': 'KSh',
2761 |             'USD': '$',
2762 |             'EUR': '€',
2763 |             'GBP': '£',
2764 |             'TZS': 'TSh',
2765 |             'UGX': 'USh'
2766 |         }
2767 | 
2768 |         currency_code = data['currency_code'].upper()
2769 |         if currency_code not in valid_currencies:
2770 |             return jsonify({
2771 |                 'error': f'Invalid currency code. Supported: {", ".join(valid_currencies.keys())}'
2772 |             }), 400
2773 | 
2774 |         # Update currency settings
2775 |         settings_service = get_services()['settings']
2776 | 
2777 |         result1 = settings_service.update_setting('currency_symbol', data['currency'], employee_id)
2778 |         result2 = settings_service.update_setting('currency_code', currency_code, employee_id)
2779 | 
2780 |         if result1['success'] and result2['success']:
2781 |             return jsonify({
2782 |                 'success': True,
2783 |                 'message': f'Currency updated to {currency_code} ({data["currency"]})',
2784 |                 'currency': data['currency'],
2785 |                 'currency_code': currency_code
2786 |             }), 200
2787 |         else:
2788 |             error_msg = result1.get('error') or result2.get('error') or 'Failed to update currency'
2789 |             return jsonify({'error': error_msg}), 400
2790 | 
2791 |     except Exception as e:
2792 |         logger.error(
2793 |             "Dashboard metrics operation failed",
2794 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
2795 |                 user_type='employee',
2796 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
2797 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent'),
2798 |                 extra={"employee_id": int(get_jwt_identity()) if get_jwt_identity() else None}
2799 |             )},
2800 |             exc_info=True,
2801 |         )
2802 |         return jsonify({'error': 'Internal server error'}), 400
2803 | 
2804 | 
2805 | @admin_bp.route('/settings/currency', methods=['GET'])
2806 | @jwt_required()
2807 | @manager_required
2808 | def get_currency_settings(current_employee):
2809 |     """
2810 |     Get current currency settings.
2811 | 
2812 |     GET /api/admin/settings/currency
2813 | 
2814 |     Returns:
2815 |         200: Current currency settings
2816 |         400: Error
2817 |     """
2818 |     try:
2819 |         settings_service = get_services()['settings']
2820 | 
2821 |         currency = settings_service.get_setting('currency_symbol')
2822 |         currency_code = settings_service.get_setting('currency_code')
2823 | 
2824 |         return jsonify({
2825 |             'success': True,
2826 |             'currency': currency or 'KSh',
2827 |             'currency_code': currency_code or 'KES'
2828 |         }), 200
2829 | 
2830 |     except Exception as e:
2831 |         logger.error(
2832 |             "Dashboard metrics operation failed",
2833 |             extra={"context": safe_auth_context(
2834 |                 user_type='employee',
2835 |                 ip=request.remote_addr,
2836 |                 user_agent=request.headers.get('User-Agent'),
2837 |                 extra={"employee_id": int(get_jwt_identity()) if get_jwt_identity() else None}
2838 |             )},
2839 |             exc_info=True,
2840 |         )
2841 |         return jsonify({'error': 'Internal server error'}), 400
2842 | 
2843 | 
2844 | # Export blueprint
2845 | __all__ = ['admin_bp']
</content>
</file>
<file><path>tests/uat_optimized.sh</path>
<content lines="1-241">
  1 | #!/bin/bash
  2 | 
  3 | #############################################
  4 | # Happy Place Webstore - OPTIMIZED UAT
  5 | # Fast End-to-End Testing Suite
  6 | #############################################
  7 | 
  8 | echo "=============================================="
  9 | echo "  HAPPY PLACE - OPTIMIZED UAT TEST SUITE"
 10 | echo "=============================================="
 11 | echo ""
 12 | 
 13 | # Configuration
 14 | BASE_URL="${BASE_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:5001/api}"
 15 | START_TIME=$(date +%s)
 16 | CURL_TIMEOUT=10  # 10 second timeout for all requests
 17 | MAX_RETRIES=2
 18 | 
 19 | # Colors
 20 | GREEN='\033[0;32m'
 21 | RED='\033[0;31m'
 22 | YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
 23 | BLUE='\033[0;34m'
 24 | NC='\033[0m'
 25 | 
 26 | # Counters
 27 | TOTAL_TESTS=0
 28 | PASSED_TESTS=0
 29 | FAILED_TESTS=0
 30 | 
 31 | # Test data
 32 | EMPLOYEE_TOKEN=""
 33 | SHIFT_ID=""
 34 | TRANSACTION_ID=""
 35 | 
 36 | #############################################
 37 | # Helper Functions
 38 | #############################################
 39 | 
 40 | # Optimized curl with timeout
 41 | api_call() {
 42 |     local method=$1
 43 |     local endpoint=$2
 44 |     local data=$3
 45 |     local token=$4
 46 |     
 47 |     local headers=(-H "Content-Type: application/json")
 48 |     
 49 |     if [ -n "$token" ]; then
 50 |         headers+=(-H "Authorization: Bearer $token")
 51 |     fi
 52 |     
 53 |     if [ "$method" = "GET" ]; then
 54 |         curl -s -m $CURL_TIMEOUT -X GET "${headers[@]}" "$BASE_URL$endpoint"
 55 |     else
 56 |         curl -s -m $CURL_TIMEOUT -X $method "${headers[@]}" -d "$data" "$BASE_URL$endpoint"
 57 |     fi
 58 | }
 59 | 
 60 | # Test result
 61 | test_result() {
 62 |     local test_name=$1
 63 |     local status=$2
 64 |     local message=$3
 65 |     
 66 |     TOTAL_TESTS=$((TOTAL_TESTS + 1))
 67 |     
 68 |     if [ "$status" = "PASS" ]; then
 69 |         echo -e "${GREEN}✓ PASS${NC} - $test_name"
 70 |         PASSED_TESTS=$((PASSED_TESTS + 1))
 71 |     else
 72 |         echo -e "${RED}✗ FAIL${NC} - $test_name: $message"
 73 |         FAILED_TESTS=$((FAILED_TESTS + 1))
 74 |     fi
 75 | }
 76 | 
 77 | #############################################
 78 | # Pre-Flight Checks
 79 | #############################################
 80 | 
 81 | echo -e "${BLUE}[PRE-FLIGHT]${NC} Checking server health..."
 82 | 
 83 | HEALTH_CHECK=$(api_call GET "/pos/health" "" "")
 84 | if echo "$HEALTH_CHECK" | grep -q "healthy"; then
 85 |     echo -e "${GREEN}✓${NC} Server is healthy"
 86 | else
 87 |     echo -e "${RED}✗ Server health check failed!${NC}"
 88 |     echo "Response: $HEALTH_CHECK"
 89 |     exit 1
 90 | fi
 91 | 
 92 | #############################################
 93 | # EMPLOYEE TESTS
 94 | #############################################
 95 | 
 96 | echo ""
 97 | echo -e "${CYAN}========================================${NC}"
 98 | echo -e "${CYAN}EMPLOYEE POS TESTS${NC}"
 99 | echo -e "${CYAN}========================================${NC}"
100 | 
101 | # TC-EMP-01: Employee Login
102 | echo -e "\n${BLUE}[TC-EMP-01]${NC} Employee Login"
103 | LOGIN_RESPONSE=$(api_call POST "/auth/employee/login" '{
104 |     "email": "manager@happyplace.co.ke",
105 |     "password": "manager123"
106 | }' "")
107 | 
108 | if echo "$LOGIN_RESPONSE" | grep -q "access_token"; then
109 |     EMPLOYEE_TOKEN=$(echo "$LOGIN_RESPONSE" | python3 -c "import sys, json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['access_token'])" 2>/dev/null)
110 |     EMPLOYEE_ID=$(echo "$LOGIN_RESPONSE" | python3 -c "import sys, json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['employee']['id'])" 2>/dev/null)
111 |     test_result "TC-EMP-01: Employee Login" "PASS" ""
112 | else
113 |     test_result "TC-EMP-01: Employee Login" "FAIL" "No access token received"
114 |     echo "Response: $LOGIN_RESPONSE"
115 |     exit 1
116 | fi
117 | 
118 | # TC-EMP-02: Get Current Shift
119 | echo -e "\n${BLUE}[TC-EMP-02]${NC} Get Current Shift"
120 | CURRENT_SHIFT=$(api_call GET "/pos/shifts/current" "" "$EMPLOYEE_TOKEN")
121 | 
122 | if echo "$CURRENT_SHIFT" | grep -q "shift_id"; then
123 |     SHIFT_ID=$(echo "$CURRENT_SHIFT" | python3 -c "import sys, json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['shift_id'])" 2>/dev/null)
124 |     test_result "TC-EMP-02: Get Current Shift" "PASS" "Shift ID: $SHIFT_ID"
125 | else
126 |     # No open shift, need to start one
127 |     echo "  No open shift found, starting new shift..."
128 |     
129 |     # TC-EMP-03: Start Shift
130 |     echo -e "\n${BLUE}[TC-EMP-03]${NC} Start New Shift"
131 |     START_SHIFT=$(api_call POST "/pos/shifts/start" '{
132 |         "store_location_id": 1,
133 |         "opening_float": 5000
134 |     }' "$EMPLOYEE_TOKEN")
135 |     
136 |     if echo "$START_SHIFT" | grep -q "shift_id"; then
137 |         SHIFT_ID=$(echo "$START_SHIFT" | python3 -c "import sys, json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['shift_id'])" 2>/dev/null)
138 |         test_result "TC-EMP-03: Start Shift" "PASS" "Shift ID: $SHIFT_ID"
139 |     else
140 |         test_result "TC-EMP-03: Start Shift" "FAIL" "Failed to start shift"
141 |         echo "Response: $START_SHIFT"
142 |         exit 1
143 |     fi
144 | fi
145 | 
146 | # TC-EMP-04: Get Products
147 | echo -e "\n${BLUE}[TC-EMP-04]${NC} Get Products for POS"
148 | PRODUCTS=$(api_call GET "/pos/products?limit=5" "" "$EMPLOYEE_TOKEN")
149 | 
150 | if echo "$PRODUCTS" | grep -q "products"; then
151 |     VARIANT_ID=$(echo "$PRODUCTS" | python3 -c "import sys, json; data=json.load(sys.stdin); print(data['products'][0]['variants'][0]['id'] if data['products'] else '')" 2>/dev/null)
152 |     test_result "TC-EMP-04: Get Products" "PASS" "Variant ID: $VARIANT_ID"
153 | else
154 |     test_result "TC-EMP-04: Get Products" "FAIL" "No products returned"
155 | fi
156 | 
157 | # TC-EMP-05: Create Transaction
158 | echo -e "\n${BLUE}[TC-EMP-05]${NC} Create POS Transaction"
159 | CREATE_TXN=$(api_call POST "/pos/transactions" '{"shift_id":'$SHIFT_ID',"payment_method":"cash","items":[{"variant_id":'$VARIANT_ID',"quantity":1}],"cash_tendered":10000}' "$EMPLOYEE_TOKEN")
160 | 
161 | if echo "$CREATE_TXN" | grep -q "transaction_id"; then
162 |     TRANSACTION_ID=$(echo "$CREATE_TXN" | python3 -c "import sys, json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['transaction_id'])" 2>/dev/null)
163 |     test_result "TC-EMP-05: Create Transaction" "PASS" "Transaction ID: $TRANSACTION_ID"
164 | else
165 |     test_result "TC-EMP-05: Create Transaction" "FAIL" "Transaction creation failed"
166 |     echo "Response: $CREATE_TXN"
167 | fi
168 | 
169 | # TC-EMP-06: Get Transaction Details
170 | echo -e "\n${BLUE}[TC-EMP-06]${NC} Get Transaction Details"
171 | if [ -n "$TRANSACTION_ID" ]; then
172 |     TXN_DETAILS=$(api_call GET "/pos/transactions/$TRANSACTION_ID" "" "$EMPLOYEE_TOKEN")
173 |     
174 |     if echo "$TXN_DETAILS" | grep -q "transaction_number"; then
175 |         test_result "TC-EMP-06: Get Transaction Details" "PASS" ""
176 |     else
177 |         test_result "TC-EMP-06: Get Transaction Details" "FAIL" "No transaction details"
178 |     fi
179 | else
180 |     test_result "TC-EMP-06: Get Transaction Details" "FAIL" "No transaction ID"
181 | fi
182 | 
183 | # TC-EMP-07: Get Shift Summary
184 | echo -e "\n${BLUE}[TC-EMP-07]${NC} Get Shift Summary"
185 | SHIFT_SUMMARY=$(api_call GET "/pos/shifts/$SHIFT_ID" "" "$EMPLOYEE_TOKEN")
186 | 
187 | if echo "$SHIFT_SUMMARY" | grep -q "shift_number"; then
188 |     test_result "TC-EMP-07: Get Shift Summary" "PASS" ""
189 | else
190 |     test_result "TC-EMP-07: Get Shift Summary" "FAIL" "No shift summary"
191 |     echo "Response: $SHIFT_SUMMARY"
192 | fi
193 | 
194 | # TC-EMP-08: Cash Movement
195 | echo -e "\n${BLUE}[TC-EMP-08]${NC} Record Cash Movement"
196 | CASH_MOVEMENT=$(api_call POST "/pos/cash-movements" '{"shift_id":'$SHIFT_ID',"movement_type":"cash_out","amount":1000,"reason":"Bank deposit"}' "$EMPLOYEE_TOKEN")
197 | 
198 | if echo "$CASH_MOVEMENT" | grep -q "success"; then
199 |     test_result "TC-EMP-08: Cash Movement" "PASS" ""
200 | else
201 |     test_result "TC-EMP-08: Cash Movement" "FAIL" "Cash movement failed"
202 | fi
203 | 
204 | # TC-EMP-09: Close Shift
205 | echo -e "\n${BLUE}[TC-EMP-09]${NC} Close Shift"
206 | CLOSE_SHIFT=$(api_call POST "/pos/shifts/$SHIFT_ID/close" '{
207 |     "closing_cash": 9000,
208 |     "notes": "UAT test shift"
209 | }' "$EMPLOYEE_TOKEN")
210 | 
211 | if echo "$CLOSE_SHIFT" | grep -q "success"; then
212 |     test_result "TC-EMP-09: Close Shift" "PASS" ""
213 | else
214 |     test_result "TC-EMP-09: Close Shift" "FAIL" "Shift close failed"
215 |     echo "Response: $CLOSE_SHIFT"
216 | fi
217 | 
218 | #############################################
219 | # FINAL REPORT
220 | #############################################
221 | 
222 | END_TIME=$(date +%s)
223 | DURATION=$((END_TIME - START_TIME))
224 | 
225 | echo ""
226 | echo "=============================================="
227 | echo "  TEST SUMMARY"
228 | echo "=============================================="
229 | echo -e "Total Tests:  ${BLUE}$TOTAL_TESTS${NC}"
230 | echo -e "Passed:       ${GREEN}$PASSED_TESTS${NC}"
231 | echo -e "Failed:       ${RED}$FAILED_TESTS${NC}"
232 | echo -e "Duration:     ${YELLOW}${DURATION}s${NC}"
233 | echo "=============================================="
234 | 
235 | if [ $FAILED_TESTS -eq 0 ]; then
236 |     echo -e "${GREEN}✓ ALL TESTS PASSED!${NC}"
237 |     exit 0
238 | else
239 |     echo -e "${RED}✗ SOME TESTS FAILED${NC}"
240 |     exit 1
241 | fi
</content>
